<script type="text/javascript">
 
        $("#toolbarCollapseSearch").on('shown.bs.collapse', function(e){
            $( '#toolbarCollapseSearch input[name="q"]' ).focus();
        });

		function CheckLogIn()
		{
			$('.btn-preloader', arguments[4]).show();
			$('[type="submit"]', arguments[4]).hide();

			var idLogin = (typeof (arguments[0]) == 'undefined') ? false : gE(arguments[0]).value;
			var idPassword = (typeof (arguments[1]) == 'undefined') ? false : gE(arguments[1]).value;
			var idAction = (typeof (arguments[2]) == 'undefined') ? false : gE(arguments[2]).value;

			if (!idLogin.length || !idPassword.length)
			{
				//alert('You should fill out both Login and Password');
				var url,
					lang = $('body').attr('data-lng'),
					accType = arguments[3];

				switch(accType)
				{
					case 'partner':
						url = "https://cabinet.instaforex.eu/partner/index.php/"+lang;
						break;

					case 'webtrader':
						url = "https://webtrader.instaforex.eu/"+ (lang == 'ru'? (lang + '/') : '') +'login';
						break;

					default:
						url = "https://cabinet.instaforex.eu/client/"+lang+'/login';
						break;
				}

				window.open(url,'_blank');

				return false;
			}

			$.post("/checkipcabinet.php",
				{
					login: idLogin,
					lang: "en",
					act: idAction
				},
				function (data)
				{
					if (typeof (data.formId) != 'undefined' && data.url.length > 0)
					{
						$(data.formId).attr("action", data.url).submit();
					}
				},
				"json"
			);

			return false;
		}

        function CabinetAuth(data) {
            $('.btn-preloader', arguments[4]).show();
            $('[type="submit"]', arguments[4]).hide();
            $('.error-auth-msg', arguments[4]).hide();

            var idLogin = (typeof (arguments[0]) == 'undefined') ? false : gE(arguments[0]).value;
            var idPassword = (typeof (arguments[1]) == 'undefined') ? false : gE(arguments[1]).value;
            var idAction = (typeof (arguments[2]) == 'undefined') ? false : gE(arguments[2]).value;

            if(idLogin !== false && idPassword !== false)
            $.post("/cabinet_auth.php",
                {
                    account: idLogin,
                    pwd: idPassword,
                    action: idAction
                },
                function (data)
                {
                    if(data.url) {
                        window.location.replace(data.url);
                    } else {
                        $('.btn-preloader', arguments[4]).hide();
                        $('[type="submit"]', arguments[4]).show();
                        $('.error-auth-msg', arguments[4]).show();
                    }
                },
                "json"
            );

            return false;
        }

	</script>

<script>

		$(document).ready(function() {
			var fastbannerform_status_tlb = true;

            if('') {
                $('input[name=iagree]').attr('checked', 'checked');
            }

			$("#toolbarCollapseOpenAccount .fastbannerform__country").select2({
				templateResult: formatState,
				templateSelection: formatState,
				dropdownParent: $('body > header')
			});

			$('body').on('click', '#toolbarCollapseOpenAccount .fastbannerform__flex__close', function(){
				$('.fastbannerform__login').hide();
				$('.fastbannerform__formcontent').show();

				$('.fastbannerform__form input[type="text"]').val('');
				$('.fastbannerform__form input[type="checkbox"]').removeAttr("checked");
			});

			$('body').on('click', '#toolbarCollapseOpenAccount .fastbannerform__flex__botton button', function(){
				var block = $(this),
					preloader = $('#toolbarCollapseOpenAccount form .btn-preloader'),
					blockform = $(this).parents('.fastbannerform__form');

	    			var fs = blockform.serialize();

				if (fs.substr(fs.indexOf('country=')+8,2)=='BE') {
					blockform.find('.fastbannerform__error, .fastbannerform__logincount, .fastbannerform__loginpassword').html("Unfortunately, the registration is unavailable for Belgian citizens.");
                			blockform.find('.fastbannerform__error').show();
					fastbannerform_status_tlb = false;
				}
				else {
					fastbannerform_status_tlb = true;
				};

				if(fastbannerform_status_tlb){
					blockform.find('.fastbannerform__flex__input input').removeClass('fastbannerform__red');

					fastbannerform_status_tlb = false;
					blockform.find('.fastbannerform__error').hide();
					blockform.find('.fastbannerform__error, .fastbannerform__logincount, .fastbannerform__loginpassword, .fastbannerform__loginserver').html('');

					$.ajax({
						url: root_http + 'fast_open_live_account.php',
						type: "POST",
						data: blockform.serialize(),
						dataType: 'json',
						beforeSend: function() {
							block.hide();
							preloader.show();
						},
						success: function(ret) {
							if (!ret.error) {
                                if(ret.redirectData.needRedirect) {
                                    window.location.replace(ret.redirectData.pageRedirect);
                                } else {
                                    $.ajax({
                                        type: "POST",
                                        url: root_http + 'fast_open_live_account.php',
                                        data: ret,
                                        dataType: 'json',
                                        success: function(ret){
                                            if(ret.data && ret.data.Login) {
                                                blockform.find('.fastbannerform__formcontent, .fastbannerform__error').hide();
                                                blockform.find('.fastbannerform__login').show();
                                                blockform.find('.fastbannerform__logincount').html(ret.data.Login);
                                                blockform.find('.fastbannerform__loginpassword').html(ret.data.Password);
                                                blockform.find('.fastbannerform__loginserver').html(ret.data.Server);

                                                if ($('[data-tokenaccount]').length > 0) {
                                                    $('[data-tokenaccount]').attr('href', $('[data-tokenaccount]').attr('href') + ret.data.Token);
                                                }

                                                ga('send', 'event', 'FastZayavka', 'Click');
                                            } else {
                                                blockform.find('.fastbannerform__error').html('Server error').show();
                                            }

                                        },
                                        error: function(){
                                            blockform.find('.fastbannerform__error').html('Server error').show();
                                        },
                                        complete: function(){
                                            block.show();
                                            preloader.hide();
                                            fastbannerform_status_tlb = true;
                                        }
                                    });
                                }
							} else {
								if (typeof ret.alert == 'object') {
									$.each(ret.alert, function(key, val) {
										blockform.find('input[name="' + key + '"]').addClass('fastbannerform__red');
										blockform.find('.fastbannerform__error').html(blockform.find('.fastbannerform__error').html() + '<div>' + val + '</div>');
									});

									blockform.find('.fastbannerform__error').show();
								} else if (typeof ret.alert == 'string') {
									blockform.find('.fastbannerform__error').html(ret.alert);
								}

								block.show();
								preloader.hide();
								fastbannerform_status_tlb = true;
							}

							var top = $('header').height() + $('.breadcrumbs').height() + $('.fastbannerform__page').height();
							$('.sidebar-left, .sidebar-right').css('top', top + 'px');
						}
					});
				}

				return false;
			});

		});

		function formatState(state){
			if(!state.id){return state.text;}
			var stateline = $('<span class="fastbannerform__span f32 NOFLAG ' + state.title + '"></span> <span class="fastbannerform__span">' + state.text + '</span>');
			return stateline;
		}
	</script>

<script>
    (function($) {
        var activereplenish = 'deposit';
        
        $(document).ready(function() {
            ActiveReplenish();
            
            $('body').on('click', '[data-replenishment_tab]', function(){
                activereplenish = $(this).data('replenishment_tab');
                
                ActiveReplenish();
            });
        });
        
        function ActiveReplenish(){
            $('[data-replenishment_tab]').removeClass('active');
            $('[data-replenishment_tab="' + activereplenish + '"]').addClass('active');
            
            $('[data-replenishment_item]').addClass('hide');
            $('[data-replenishment_item="' + activereplenish + '"]').removeClass('hide');
        }
    })(jQuery);
</script>

<script src="https://fxn.instaforex.eu/i/js/site/lib/bootstrap/collapse.js" type="text/javascript"></script>
<script>

	$(document).ready(function(){

		/**
		 * input Search focusIn
		 */
		$('body > header #toolbarCollapseSearch input[type="search"]').focusin(function() {
			$('button[type="submit"]', $(this).parent()).addClass('active');
		});
		/**
		 * input Search focusOut
		 */
		$('body > header #toolbarCollapseSearch input[type="search"]').focusout(function() {
			$('button[type="submit"]', $(this).parent()).removeClass('active');
		});

		/**
		 * all toolbarCollapse before show
		 */
		$('[id^="toolbarCollapse"]').on('show.bs.collapse', function () {
			toolbarHideAllCollapse();
			closeNMenu();
		});

		$('#toolbarCollapsePartnerCabinet .main-block > button').on('click', function (e) {
			$('#toolbarCollapsePartnerCabinet .main-block > button').not($(this)).removeClass('active');
			$(this).addClass('active');

			$('#toolbarCollapsePartnerCabinet .main-block > .collapse').hide();
			$('#' + $(this).data('target')).show();

			$('#toolbarCollapsePartnerCabinet .promo-block > div').removeClass('active');
			$('#toolbarCollapsePartnerCabinet .promo-block > div[data-id="' + $(this).data('target') + '"]').addClass('active');

			$('#toolbarCollapsePartnerCabinet .image-block > img').attr('src', $(this).data('image-block_image'));

		});

		/**
		 * click вне тулбара
		 */
		$(document).mouseup(function(e){
			if($('body > header').has(e.target).length === 0 && $(e.target).parents('.select2-container').length === 0 && $('.fancybox-overlay').has(e.target).length === 0){
				toolbarHideAllCollapse();
			}
		});

		/**
		 * Скрывать туулбар, если проматали несколько экранов вниз
		 */
		window.onscroll = function() {
			if(window.pageYOffset > ($('body > header').height() + (3 * $(window).height()))){
				toolbarHideAllCollapse();
			}
		};

		/**
		 * mobile menu_icon click
		 */
		$('button.main_nav_menu_icon').on('click', function(e){
			e.preventDefault();
			collapseNMenu();
		});

		/**
		 *
		 */
		$('#main_nav_menu > ul > li > a[data-target]').on('click', function(e) {
			e.preventDefault();

			var block = $(this).parent().parent();
			var next_block = $('#main_nav_menu > ul[data-name="' + $(this).data('target') + '"]');

			// анемируем следующий
			next_block.addClass('active animated slideInRight');
			// анимируем текущий
			block.addClass('closing animated slideOutLeft');
			setTimeout(function(){
				// скрываем текущий
				block.removeClass('closing active animated slideOutLeft');
				// убираем анимацию со следующего
				next_block.removeClass('animated slideInRight');
			}, 800);

		});

	});

	function collapseNMenu(){
		if(!$('body > header .nheader button.main_nav_menu_icon').hasClass('active')){
			closeNMenu();
		} else {
			showNMenu();
		}
	}

	function showNMenu(){
		toolbarHideAllCollapse();
		$('body > header .nheader button.main_nav_menu_icon').addClass('active');
		$('#main_nav_menu').addClass('active');
	}

	function closeNMenu(){
		$('body > header .nheader button.main_nav_menu_icon').removeClass('active');
		$('#main_nav_menu').removeClass('active');
	}

	function toolbarHideAllCollapse(){
		$('[id^="toolbarCollapse"]').collapse('hide');
	}

</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
        $(document).ready(function() {
            var errorNotEU = "Registration is not available for non-EU residents. Please, proceed to the website of the other member of the InstaForex group.";
            $('.mainopenaccount__error').show();
            $(".mainopenaccount__country").select2()
                .on("select2:select", function (e) {
                    var euCountry = ["AUT","ITA","BEL","BGR","LTU","HRV","LUX","CYP","MLT","CZE","NLD","DNK","POL","EST","PRT","FIN","ROU","FRA","DEU","SVN","GRC","ESP","HUN","SWE","IRL","GBR", "ISL", "LIE", "NOR"];
                    var selected_element = $(e.currentTarget);
                    var select_val = selected_element.val();
                    if (euCountry.indexOf(select_val) === -1) {
                        $('.mainopenaccount__error').show();
                        $('.mainopenaccount__error').text(errorNotEU);
                    } else {
                        $('.mainopenaccount__error').hide();
                    }
                });
        });
    </script>
<script>
    var onSlideAfterWidth = 0; 

    $(document).ready(function(){
        //        if(isMobile.any())
//            $('#block-bonuses-content > a.bonus:last').show();

        var analytics_dropdown_text=$('.drop-down:first-child ul li:last-child').text().trim();
        $('.analytics-text_right').text(analytics_dropdown_text);
        var instrument_dropdown_text=$('.drop-down:nth-child(2) ul li:last-child').text().trim();
        $('.instrument-text_right').text(instrument_dropdown_text);

        
        $('#block-bonuses-content a.bonus').on('click', function(e) {
            var screen_width = $(window).width();    

            if(screen_width > 900) {
                return true;
            }
            
            e.preventDefault();         
            $('#block-bonuses-content a.bonus').removeClass('open');
            $(this).addClass('open');

        });
        
        $('#block-bonuses-content a.bonus .title').on('click', function(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            
            var link = $(this).parents('a').prop('href');
            window.open(link);
        });

        var slider = $('#slides .bxslider').bxSlider({
            mode: 'fade',
            pause: 7000,
            speed: 1000,
            autoStart: true,
            auto: true,
            autoControls: false,
            autoHover: false,
            onSlideAfter: function() {
                slider.stopAuto();
                slider.startAuto();
            }
        });

		$('.slides2 ul').bxSlider({
					  controls: false,
					  slideWidth: 1190,
					  height: 590,
					  auto: true,
					  pause: 10000,
					});


    	$('.slides2 ul li').css({"visibility":"visible"}); // показываем невидимые слайды после формирования слайдера
    	$('.slides2 .loader').css({"display":"none"});

        $('.forex_news_fancybox' , $(this)).fancybox({
            type:'ajax',
            maxWidth	: 800,
            maxHeight	: 600,
            width		: '70%',
            height		: '70%',
            autoSize	: true
        });

		$('#scrollspy').scrollSpy({offset: $('body > header').height()});

                var width_display = '';
                $('.div_block_img, h3.info-support').click(function(){
                    width_display = window.innerWidth;
                    if(width_display < 769)
                    {
                        var target = $(this).parent().find('.hr a.toggle-btn');
                        target.trigger('click');
                    }
                });

		$('.block .hr .toggle-btn').click(function(e) {
			e.preventDefault();
			var $target = $($(this).attr('href'));
                        
                        var parent_block = $target.parent();
                        var first_icon_parent = $(parent_block).children().children('.first_img');
                        var second_icon_parent = $(parent_block).children().children('.second_img');
                        
                        first_icon_parent.show();
                        second_icon_parent.hide();
                        
			if(!$target.is(":visible")) {
                            first_icon_parent.hide();
                            second_icon_parent.show();
				var $parent = $target.parent(),
					offsetTop = $parent.offset().top;
				$('html, body')
						.stop()
						.animate({scrollTop: offsetTop}, 400, 'easeInOutQuint');
			}

			$target
//				.stop()
				.slideToggle();

			return false;
		});


		function getMonitoringWidgets() {
			var block = $('#monitoring_widgets'),
				url = 'https://www.instaforex.eu/widget_monitoring.php',
				promise = $.get(url);

			promise
				.done(success)
				.fail(error);

			function success(result) {
				block.html(result);
				$('#monitoring').tabs();
				$('#position').tabs();
			}

			function error() {
				console.log('error');
			}
		}

		function getMonitoringWidgetsNew() {
			var block = $('#monitoring_widgets'),
				url = 'https://www.instaforex.eu/forexcopy_top5';

			$.ajax({
                url: url,
                type: 'get',
                data: {action: 'get_top_traders_main'},
                success: function(result) {
                    block.html(result);
                },
                error: function() {
                    console.log('error');
                }
            });
		}

        getMonitoringWidgetsNew();

		var connect_block_timeout = setTimeout(connect_block_show, 5 * 60 * 1000);
        
		function connect_block_show() {
			$('#connect_block').addClass('show_links');
		}

		$(document).on('mousemove.connect_block', function (e) {
			if ($('#connect_block').css("opacity") == '0' && $('#connect_block').css("display") != 'none') {
				var X = e.pageX; // положения по оси X
				if ($("html").width() - X < 200) {
					connect_block_show();
					clearTimeout(connect_block_timeout);
				}
			}
		});
	});
</script>

<script>

    $(document).ready( function(){

        $('#news').uploading_tabs({loadEveryTime: true, setRandomTab: true});

        $('.click_for_bxslider').one('click', function(){

            $('.ism_content').each(function(index, item) {
                var 
                    elem = $(item),
                    url = elem.data('url');
                    if(!url) return false;
                $.ajax({
                    url: url,
                    success: function(msg) {
                        if(!msg){
                            elem.parent().remove();
                            return false;
                        }
                        elem.html(msg);
                        if(elem.data('bxslider')) {
                            elem.find('ul').bxSlider({
                                controls: false,
                                adaptiveHeight: true
                            });
                        }
                    },
                    error: function() {
                        elem.parent().remove();
                    }
                });
            });
        });
    });
    
    </script>

            $('#video').uploading_tabs({loadEveryTime: true, setRandomTab: true});

            var url_interview = 'https://www.instaforex.eu/video-interview.php?table_for_main=1';
            var interview_content = $('.interview_content');
            
            $.ajax({
                url: url_interview,
                success: function(data) {
                    if(!data){
                        interview_content.remove();
                        return false;
                    }
                    interview_content.html(data);
                },
                error: function() {
                    interview_content.remove();
                }
            });
            
            $('.click_itm_content').one('click', function(){
                $('.itm_content').each(function(index, item) {
                    var 
                        elem = $(item),
                        url = elem.data('url');
                    if(!url) return false;
                    $.ajax({
                        url: url,
                        success: function(msg) {
                            if(!msg){
                                elem.parent().remove();
                                return false;
                            }
                            elem.html(msg);
                        },
                        error: function() {
                            elem.parent().remove();

<script>
	$(document).ready(function() {

        if ( $(window).width() < 769 ) {
            var is_opened = false;
            $('.drop-down').on('click', function() {
                var ul = $(this).find('ul');
                if (is_opened) {
                    ul.hide();
                    is_opened = false;
                } else {
                    ul.show();
                    is_opened = true;
                }
            });
        }

        if('') {
            $('input[name=iagree]').attr('checked', 'checked');
        }

		setTimeout(function() {
            if ($(window).width() > 700) {
				$("#block-contests-content .tiles .tile")
					.each(function(el) {

						var p = $(this).find('p');
						p
							.data('height', p.height())
							//.data('height', 40)
							.css({height: 0, opacity: 0.0});

							$(this).hover(
								function () {
									p.stop().animate({height: p.data('height'), opacity: 1.0}, 200);
								},
								function () {
									p.stop().animate({height: 0, opacity: 0.0}, 200);
								}
							);

				});
			}
        }, 1000);
    });
</script>

<script>
	function receiveSize(e) {
		if (e && e.data) try {
			var t = JSON.parse(e.data);
			if (t.id && t.id == "inf_quotes_chart")
			{ document.getElementById("inf_quotes_chart").style.height = t.height + 20 + "px" }
		} catch (a) {
		}
	}
	("Microsoft Internet Explorer" != navigator.appName || ver >= 8) && window.addEventListener("message", receiveSize, !1);
</script>

<script>
                (function ($) {
                    $('#footer-menu h4').click(function (e){
                        var footerblock = $(this).parents('.footerblock__one');
                        
                        if(footerblock.hasClass('open')){
                            $('.footerblock__one').removeClass('open');
                            $('.footerblock__one').find('ul').slideUp();
                        } else {
                            $('.footerblock__one').removeClass('open');
                            $('.footerblock__one').find('ul').slideUp();
                            
                            footerblock.addClass('open');
                            footerblock.find('ul').slideDown();
                        }
                    });
                })(jQuery);

                $(document).ready(function(){
                    var count_click = 0;
                    
                    $(document).on('click','.flag_holder', function (e) {
                        count_click +=1;
                        var mobile_device = $(window).width();
                        
                        if(mobile_device  < 991){
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            e.preventDefault();
                            
                            if(count_click >1){
                                $(this).parent('div').find('ul').toggle();
                            } else {
                                $(this).parent('div').find('ul').css('display','flex');
                            }
                        }
                    });

                    $(document).on('click', function (event) {
                        var mobile_device = $(window).width();
                        
                        if(mobile_device  < 991){
                            $(".flag_holder").parent('div').find('ul').css('display','none');
                        }
                    });
                });

		</script>

<script>
    var sidebar_part_1 = '<div flex="0" flex-box="1" flex-order="1" class="contentflex__center__left boxsizing__box" align-self-one="stretch"><div class="contentflex__noposition contentflex__noposition__1 boxsizing__box"></div></div>',
        sidebar_part_2 = '<div flex="0" flex-box="1" flex-order="3" class="contentflex__center__right boxsizing__box" align-self-one="stretch"><div class="contentflex__noposition contentflex__noposition__2 boxsizing__box"></div></div>',
        conteiner_1 = '<div class="flex-container contentflex__center boxsizing__box" flex-wrap="nowrap" flex-justify="center"></div>', /* общая обертка */
        conteiner_2 = '<div flex="1" flex-box="1" flex-order="2" class="contentflex__center__center boxsizing__box" align-self-one="stretch"></div>', /* 1-я обертка центрального блока */
        conteiner_3 = '<div class="contentflex__noposition contentflex__noposition__3 boxsizing__box"></div>'; /* 2-я обертка центрального блока */

    $(document).ready(function() {
        var sidebar_addblock = $('.page .block.sections:first');

        if (sidebar_addblock.length > 0) {
            /* обертовываем основной контейнер */
            sidebar_addblock.wrapInner(conteiner_3);
            sidebar_addblock.wrapInner(conteiner_2);

            /* добавляем левый и правый блоки */
            sidebar_addblock.append(sidebar_part_1);
            sidebar_addblock.append(sidebar_part_2);
            
            /* обертовываем все что добавили */
            sidebar_addblock.wrapInner(conteiner_1);
            
            sidebar_addblock.find('.contentflex__center__left .contentflex__noposition').html($('.sidebar.sidebar-left'));
            sidebar_addblock.find('.contentflex__center__right .contentflex__noposition').html($('.sidebar.sidebar-right'));

            $('.sidebar', sidebar_addblock).css('display', 'block').css('top', '0px');
        } else {
            $('.sidebar.sidebar-left').remove();
            $('.sidebar.sidebar-right').remove();
        }
    });
</script>
<script>
	$(function () {

			scrollName: 'scrollup',

    <!-- Подстраивает сайдбары  -->
    function do_sidebar(){
        var content_block = $('.page .block.sections').first();
        if(content_block.length > 0){
            if(!sidebarnew){
                content_block.css('position', 'relative');
                content_block.append($('.sidebar.sidebar-left')).append($('.sidebar.sidebar-right'));
                
                $('.sidebar', content_block).css('display', 'block').css('top', '0px');
            }
        }
    }
    
	$(document).ready(function() {
        do_sidebar();
    
		var cv = '';
		var cvGet = '';
		cv = (cvGet !== '') ? cvGet : cv;
		if (cv !== '') {
			sendVisitData(cv);
			setInterval(function(){
				sendVisitData(cv);
			}, 20000);
		}
	});

	function sendVisitData(cv)
	{
		var is_google = '';
		var ref = document.referrer;
		var google_ref = (~ref.indexOf('google.')) ? 1 : 0;
		$.ajax({
				method: 'post',
				url: 'https://www.instaforex.eu/visit-stat.php',
				data: {cv: cv, se: is_google, gf: google_ref},
				success: function(data) {

				}
			}
		);
	}
</script>

<script>
        var numberScrollTop = 0;

        function openMobileMenu(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            numberScrollTop = window.scrollY;
            document.getElementById('menu_mobile_block').style.cssText = 'display: block; top:' + numberScrollTop + 'px; height:' + window.innerHeight + 'px;';
            document.body.setAttribute('class', document.body.getAttribute('class') + ' menu_mobile_active');
            document.body.style.cssText = 'width:' + window.innerWidth + 'px; height:' + window.innerHeight + 'px; margin-top:' + (-(numberScrollTop)) + 'px;';
            setTimeout(function () {
                document.body.style.height = window.innerHeight + 'px';
                document.getElementById('menu_mobile_block_background').style.opacity = '.7';
                document.getElementById('menu_mobile').style.left = '-5px';
            }, 50);
        }

        function resizeMobileMenu() {
            if (document.body.getAttribute('class').search(/menu_mobile_active/i) !== -1) {
                document.getElementById('menu_mobile_block').style.height = window.innerHeight + 'px';
                document.body.style.width = window.innerWidth + 'px';
                document.body.style.height = window.innerHeight + 'px';
            }
        }

        function closeMobileMenu(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            var body = document.body;
            var menu_mobile_block_background = document.getElementById('menu_mobile_block_background');
            var menu_mobile = document.getElementById('menu_mobile');
            var classBody = document.body.getAttribute('class').replace(/(\s{2}|(\s*menu_mobile_active)|^\s|\s$)/g, '');
            var style = getComputedStyle(menu_mobile_block_background);

            body.style.position = 'fixed';
            body.setAttribute('class', classBody);
            menu_mobile.style.left = '-280px';
            menu_mobile_block_background.style.opacity = '0';

            setTimeout(function () {
                document.getElementById('menu_mobile_block').setAttribute('style', '');
                document.body.setAttribute('style', '');
                window.scrollTo(0, numberScrollTop);
            }, parseFloat(style.transitionDuration) * 1000);
        }

        function readyToStartMenu() {
            $('#mobile_menu_button').on('click', function (e) {
                openMobileMenu(e);
            });

            $('#menu_mobile_block_background').on('click', function (e) {
                closeMobileMenu(e);
            });

            $('#menu_mobile').on('click', '.menu_parent > a', function (e) {
                e.preventDefault();
                var arrayLinks = document.querySelectorAll('#menu_mobile > ul > li.menu_parent:not(.menu_important)');
                var $target = $(e.target);
                var $parent = $target.parent();
                var has = $parent.hasClass('active');

                for (var i = 0, size = arrayLinks.length; i < size; i++) {
                    if ($(arrayLinks[i]).hasClass('active')) {
                        $(arrayLinks[i]).removeClass('active').find('>div').eq(0).slideUp("fast", function () {
                            // Animation complete.
                        });
                    }
                }
                if (has) {
                    $parent.removeClass('active').find('>div').eq(0).slideUp("fast", function () {
                        // Animation complete.
                    });
                } else {
                    $parent.addClass('active').find('>div').eq(0).slideDown("fast", function () {
                        // Animation complete.
                    });
                }
            });
        }

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', readyToStartMenu);
        window.addEventListener('resize', resizeMobileMenu);

        document.addEventListener('touchstart', handleTouchStart, false);
        document.addEventListener('touchend', handleTouchStop, false);

        var xTouch = null;
        var yTouch = null;

        function handleTouchStart(e) {
            xTouch = e.touches[0].clientX;
            yTouch = e.touches[0].clientY;
        }

        function handleTouchStop(e) {
            if (!xTouch || !yTouch) {
                return;
            }

            var xUp = e.changedTouches[0].clientX;
            var yUp = e.changedTouches[0].clientY;

            var xDiff = xTouch - xUp;
            var yDiff = yTouch - yUp;

            if (Math.abs(xDiff) > Math.abs(yDiff)) {/*most significant*/
                if (xDiff > 0) {
                    /* left swipe */
                    if ($('body').hasClass('menu_mobile_active') && Math.abs(xDiff) > document.documentElement.clientWidth / 5 && Math.abs(yDiff) < 100) {
                        closeMobileMenu(e);
                    }
                } else {
                    /* right swipe */
                    if (!$('body').hasClass('menu_mobile_active') && Math.abs(xDiff) > document.documentElement.clientWidth / 2 && Math.abs(yDiff) < 100) {
                        openMobileMenu(e);
                    }
                }
            } else {
                if (yDiff > 0) {
                    /* up swipe */
                } else {
                    /* down swipe */
                }
            }
            /* reset values */
            xDown = null;
            yDown = null;
        }
    </script>
